HyperFrames Starter Prompts | By Janus Tiu
HyperFrames Starter Prompts
Hello friends, I am Janus Tiu, thanks for checking it out.
These are starter prompts you can use to create motion graphics by vibe coding with HyperFrames,  an open-source video rendering framework by HeyGen.
How to get started
Install HyperFrames as a skill in your AI coding agent (Cursor, Claude Code, etc.):
npx skills add heygen-com/hyperframes
Start prompting using any of the examples below.
You can learn more and explore the full docs here: github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes
Starter prompts
Product Demos
Using /hyperframes, create a 15-second product intro: fade in the app name, show 3 key features as animated text cards with slide-up transitions, and end with a CTA screen. Use a clean, minimal style.
Using /hyperframes, create a 10-second app walkthrough with a phone mockup in the center, screen transitions that swipe left between 3 screens, and a subtle background pulse animation.
Social Content
Make a 9:16 TikTok-style hook video about [your topic] using /hyperframes, with bouncy captions synced to a TTS narration.
Using /hyperframes, create a 12-second Instagram carousel-style video: 4 slides with bold headlines that scale in, each with a 3-second hold, smooth crossfade between slides, dark background with white text.
Data & Infographics
Turn this CSV into an animated bar chart race using /hyperframes. Use bold colors, label each bar, and add a running counter in the corner.
Using /hyperframes, create a 20-second animated infographic comparing [A] vs [B] — start with a split screen, animate stats counting up on each side, and end with a winner reveal.
Portfolio & Presentations
Using /hyperframes, create a 30-second portfolio reel: fade in my name and title, then cycle through 5 project images with a Ken Burns zoom effect, ending with a contact CTA.
Summarize the attached PDF into a 45-second pitch video using /hyperframes. Use key stats as animated text overlays with a professional blue color palette. UI Design System Master Prompt  
Hello friends, I am Janus Tiu, thanks for checking it out. 
This is the exact prompt template I use to keep my vibe-coded projects visually consistent from start to finish. It defines your colors, typography, spacing, and component patterns in a format AI tools actually follow.
How to use it:
Copy the template below
Fill in your own values (or use the example as a starting point)
Paste it at the beginning of your project before writing any code
Save it as design-system.md in your project folder for easy reference
Add examples when things drift - if the AI starts making inconsistent choices, paste a screenshot or describe a component that looks right: "The buttons on the home screen are correct. Match that style."
The Prompt 
You are building a [type of app] with a [aesthetic style] visual identity.
## Color Palette
- Primary: [hex] — used for main actions, key UI elements
- Secondary: [hex] — used for supporting elements, secondary buttons
- Background: [hex] — main canvas color
- Surface: [hex] — cards, modals, elevated elements
- Text Primary: [hex] — headings, body copy
- Text Secondary: [hex] — captions, helper text, placeholders
- Border: [hex] — dividers, input outlines
- Success: [hex]
- Warning: [hex]
- Error: [hex]
## Typography
- Font Family: [font name from Google Fonts]
- Headings: bold, tracking tight
- Body: regular weight
- Size Scale: 12 / 14 / 16 / 20 / 24 / 32 / 40 / 48px
## Spacing Scale
Use a 4px base unit. Common values: 4, 8, 12, 16, 24, 32, 48, 64px
## Border Radius
- Small (inputs, chips): [x]px
- Medium (cards, buttons): [x]px
- Large (modals, containers): [x]px
- Full (avatars, pills): 9999px
## Shadows
- Subtle: 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)
- Medium: 0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)
- Strong: 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.15)
## Component Patterns
Buttons: [describe padding, height, hover states]
Inputs: [describe height, padding, focus states]
Cards: [describe padding, shadow, border treatment]
## Rules
1. Never introduce colors outside this palette
2. Always use the spacing scale — no arbitrary values
3. Maintain consistent border radius per element type
4. When in doubt, add more whitespace
Filled Example (Retro Groovy)
You are building a habit tracker app with a retro groovy visual identity.
## Color Palette
- Primary: #E85D04 — warm orange for main actions
- Secondary: #9D4EDD — electric purple for accents
- Background: #FFF8F0 — warm cream
- Surface: #FFFFFF
- Text Primary: #1A1A1A
- Text Secondary: #6B6B6B
- Border: #E0D5C7
- Success: #2D6A4F
- Warning: #E85D04
- Error: #D00000
## Typography
- Font Family: Space Grotesk
- Headings: bold, tracking tight
- Body: regular weight
- Size Scale: 12 / 14 / 16 / 20 / 24 / 32 / 40 / 48px
## Spacing Scale
4px base. Use: 4, 8, 12, 16, 24, 32, 48, 64px
## Border Radius
- Small: 4px
- Medium: 12px
- Large: 24px
- Full: 9999px
## Shadows
- Subtle: 0 2px 4px rgba(232, 93, 4, 0.08)
- Medium: 0 4px 16px rgba(232, 93, 4, 0.12)
## Component Patterns
Buttons: 16px vertical / 24px horizontal padding, 48px height, scale to 1.02 on hover
Inputs: 48px height, 16px padding, 2px border on focus using primary color
Cards: 24px padding, medium shadow, medium radius
## Rules
1. Never introduce colors outside this palette
2. Always use the spacing scale
3. Buttons and interactive elements get the playful scale hover
4. Maintain generous whitespace — this aesthetic breathes
UI Design System Master Prompt  
Hello friends, I am Janus Tiu, thanks for checking it out. 
This is the exact prompt template I use to keep my vibe-coded projects visually consistent from start to finish. It defines your colors, typography, spacing, and component patterns in a format AI tools actually follow.
How to use it:
Copy the template below
Fill in your own values (or use the example as a starting point)
Paste it at the beginning of your project before writing any code
Save it as design-system.md in your project folder for easy reference
Add examples when things drift - if the AI starts making inconsistent choices, paste a screenshot or describe a component that looks right: "The buttons on the home screen are correct. Match that style."
The Prompt 
You are building a [type of app] with a [aesthetic style] visual identity.
## Color Palette
- Primary: [hex] — used for main actions, key UI elements
- Secondary: [hex] — used for supporting elements, secondary buttons
- Background: [hex] — main canvas color
- Surface: [hex] — cards, modals, elevated elements
- Text Primary: [hex] — headings, body copy
- Text Secondary: [hex] — captions, helper text, placeholders
- Border: [hex] — dividers, input outlines
- Success: [hex]
- Warning: [hex]
- Error: [hex]
## Typography
- Font Family: [font name from Google Fonts]
- Headings: bold, tracking tight
- Body: regular weight
- Size Scale: 12 / 14 / 16 / 20 / 24 / 32 / 40 / 48px
## Spacing Scale
Use a 4px base unit. Common values: 4, 8, 12, 16, 24, 32, 48, 64px
## Border Radius
- Small (inputs, chips): [x]px
- Medium (cards, buttons): [x]px
- Large (modals, containers): [x]px
- Full (avatars, pills): 9999px
## Shadows
- Subtle: 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)
- Medium: 0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)
- Strong: 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.15)
## Component Patterns
Buttons: [describe padding, height, hover states]
Inputs: [describe height, padding, focus states]
Cards: [describe padding, shadow, border treatment]
## Rules
1. Never introduce colors outside this palette
2. Always use the spacing scale — no arbitrary values
3. Maintain consistent border radius per element type
4. When in doubt, add more whitespace

HyperFrames Starter Prompts | By Janus Tiu
HyperFrames Starter Prompts
Hello friends, I am Janus Tiu, thanks for checking it out.
These are starter prompts you can use to create motion graphics by vibe coding with HyperFrames,  an open-source video rendering framework by HeyGen.
How to get started
Install HyperFrames as a skill in your AI coding agent (Cursor, Claude Code, etc.):
npx skills add heygen-com/hyperframes
Start prompting using any of the examples below.
You can learn more and explore the full docs here: github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes
Starter prompts
Product Demos
Using /hyperframes, create a 15-second product intro: fade in the app name, show 3 key features as animated text cards with slide-up transitions, and end with a CTA screen. Use a clean, minimal style.
Using /hyperframes, create a 10-second app walkthrough with a phone mockup in the center, screen transitions that swipe left between 3 screens, and a subtle background pulse animation.
Social Content
Make a 9:16 TikTok-style hook video about [your topic] using /hyperframes, with bouncy captions synced to a TTS narration.
Using /hyperframes, create a 12-second Instagram carousel-style video: 4 slides with bold headlines that scale in, each with a 3-second hold, smooth crossfade between slides, dark background with white text.
Data & Infographics
Turn this CSV into an animated bar chart race using /hyperframes. Use bold colors, label each bar, and add a running counter in the corner.
Using /hyperframes, create a 20-second animated infographic comparing [A] vs [B] — start with a split screen, animate stats counting up on each side, and end with a winner reveal.
Portfolio & Presentations
Using /hyperframes, create a 30-second portfolio reel: fade in my name and title, then cycle through 5 project images with a Ken Burns zoom effect, ending with a contact CTA.
Summarize the attached PDF into a 45-second pitch video using /hyperframes. Use key stats as animated text overlays with a professional blue color palette.
Prompts I used in my demo:
Shape Morph Ring Animation
Using /hyperframes, create a 5-second seamless looping video (1080x1920, 30fps) of a shape-morphing ring animation centered on screen. Background is a soft mint green (#B8E6C8). The shapes are a deep forest green (#1B4332).
Setup: 8 identical shapes are arranged in a circle around the center point, evenly spaced at 45° intervals. Each shape is positioned on the ring at a consistent radius from center. The entire ring rotates slowly and continuously clockwise (one full rotation takes ~10 seconds, so across the 5-second loop it completes a half turn). Each individual shape also rotates on its own axis, always facing tangentially along the ring.
The morph sequence plays continuously across the 5 seconds. The shapes transition smoothly through these geometric forms in order, blending fluidly between each:
Phase 1 (0s–0.6s) — Diamonds/Rosette: 8 shapes start as a mix of squares and 45°-rotated diamonds arranged tightly, edges nearly touching, forming an 8-pointed star cutout in the negative space at center. Alternating shapes are rotated 45° relative to their neighbors.
Phase 2 (0.6s–1.2s) — Organic Blobs: Corners round dramatically. Shapes become soft organic blobs with high border-radius. They overlap slightly, creating a scalloped flower ring.
Phase 3 (1.2s–1.8s) — Teardrops/Petals: Shapes elongate into teardrop forms, pointed end facing center, round end facing outward. Arranged in a pinwheel — each petal tilted slightly clockwise, creating a spinning flower/windmill pattern.
Phase 4 (1.8s–2.5s) — Leaves: Shapes shrink slightly and morph into leaf/marquise shapes (pointed at both ends). The ring radius expands so there are visible gaps between each leaf. Individual rotation continues.
Phase 5 (2.5s–3.2s) — Semicircles: Shapes morph into half-circles, flat edge facing inward, round edge facing outward. Arranged in a gear-like ring pattern. Ring radius contracts slightly.
Phase 6 (3.2s–3.8s) — Rounded Triangles: Shapes become guitar-pick / rounded-triangle forms, still rotating individually, creating a turbine-like pattern.
Phase 7 (3.8s–4.3s) — Squares: Shapes grow into larger rounded squares, spread out on a wider ring radius. Each square is slightly rotated on its own axis, creating a loose octagonal arrangement. A faint thin circular guide line may be visible behind the shapes.
Phase 8 (4.3s–5.0s) — Arrows: Shapes morph into arrow/chevron shapes pointing inward toward center, tightly packed, forming a starburst/snowflake pattern with a star-shaped cutout in the negative space. This transitions seamlessly back to Phase 1 for the loop.
Key motion principles:
All shape morphs use smooth easing (power2.inOut) — no hard cuts between forms
The ring rotation is constant and linear (no easing) for a hypnotic feel
Individual shape rotation is slightly faster than ring rotation
The ring radius subtly breathes — expanding during looser arrangements (leaves, squares) and contracting during tighter ones (rosette, arrows)
The morph between shapes should animate border-radius, scale, aspect ratio, and rotation simultaneously
Implementation note: Each of the 8 shapes can be a single div with CSS transforms (translate for ring position, rotate for individual angle) and animated border-radius / width / height / clip-path for the shape morphing. Use GSAP timeline with morphSVG or clip-path interpolation for the shape transitions. Export as MP4 for Instagram Reel.
Infinite Horizontal Carousel
Using /hyperframes, create a 5-second seamless looping video (1080x1080, 30fps) of an infinite horizontal carousel showcasing Instagram post thumbnails inside phone-shaped frames.
Background: Soft warm off-white (#F0EDE8), flat, no texture.
Card structure: 3 phone-shaped cards are visible at all times, arranged horizontally across the center of the frame. Each card is a tall rounded rectangle (aspect ratio approximately 9:16, roughly 320px wide × 570px tall at base scale) with large rounded corners (24px radius). Each card displays one of the user-provided Instagram thumbnail images as its full-bleed background, scaled to cover/fill the card with object-fit: cover.
Card hierarchy — center vs sides:
The center card is the hero: scaled up slightly (scale: 1.05), vertically centered in the frame, with a subtle phone notch detail (a small centered pill shape, ~60px wide × 6px tall, dark semi-transparent) near the top edge inside the card, and a faint drop shadow (0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.12)).
The side cards (left and right) are slightly smaller (scale: 0.95), shifted down ~8px from center, no notch detail, and slightly lower opacity (0.9). They are partially cropped by the frame edges — about 30–40% of each side card is clipped off-screen.
Gap between cards: ~24px spacing.
Animation — constant horizontal scroll:
All cards translate continuously to the left at a constant linear speed (no easing — smooth, machine-like drift).
The scroll speed is calibrated so that one full card-width passes every ~1.7 seconds — across the 5-second loop, all thumbnails cycle through the center position and the final frame matches the first frame exactly for a seamless loop.
As a card exits the left edge, it reappears on the right edge (infinite scroll behavior). Duplicate the card set so there are always cards entering from the right to fill the frame.
The scale, opacity, shadow, and notch of each card should animate smoothly as it transitions between "side" and "center" positions — scaling up as it approaches center, scaling back down as it leaves. Use a smooth power2.inOut ease for the scale/opacity transitions tied to X position.
Thumbnail slots: The composition should support [INSERT NUMBER] thumbnail images. Each image fills its card completely as a background. User will provide the actual image files — use placeholder colored rectangles during first draft (use distinct colors per card so positions are easy to track: e.g., card 1 = coral #FF6B6B, card 2 = sky blue #4ECDC4, card 3 = warm yellow #FFE66D, card 4 = lavender #A8A4CE, etc.).
Mood: Clean, professional, satisfying — like a polished portfolio presentation or an app store feature carousel. The motion should feel effortless and continuous, not snappy or rushed. No text overlays on the cards, no UI chrome beyond the phone notch. No voiceover. Export as MP4 for Instagram Reel.

Here is a **complete extraction + deep system-level expansion** of the image you provided.
# ✅ **EXTRACTED CONTENT (FROM IMAGE)**
## **“8 ChatGPT Prompt Frameworks To Help You Master AI”**
## **1. R-T-F (Role – Task – Format)**
**R (Role):** Specify the role you want the AI to assume
**T (Task):** Define the task you want AI to perform
**F (Format):** Indicate the output format
**Example:**
* Role → Act as a brand strategist
* Task → Write a messaging hierarchy for a B2B SaaS founder targeting finance teams
* Format → Bullet points with core message, value props, proof, and CTAs
## **2. S-O-L-V-E (Situation – Objective – Limitations – Vision – Execution)**
**S (Situation):** Define the context
**O (Objective):** Specify goals
**L (Limitations):** Define constraints
**V (Vision):** Outline long-term goal
**E (Execution):** Plan steps
**Example:**
* Situation → Leading content team at startup launching product
* Objective → Generate qualified inbound leads
* Limitations → No ad spend, small team
* Vision → Become go-to thought leader
* Execution → Weekly content + double down on best content
## **3. T-A-G (Task – Action – Goal)**
**T (Task):** Define task
**A (Action):** State action needed
**G (Goal):** Clarify desired outcome
**Example:**
* Task → Reduce customer churn
* Action → Analyze churn + retention program
* Goal → Improve retention by 15% in 6 months
## **4. R-A-C-E (Role – Action – Context – Expectation)**
**R (Role):** Character AI plays
**A (Action):** What to do
**C (Context):** Background info
**E (Expectation):** Desired output
**Example:**
* Role → B2B consultant
* Action → Build outreach framework
* Context → Workflow software for logistics firms
* Expectation → 3 templates tailored to buyer roles
## **5. D-R-E-A-M (Define – Research – Execute – Analyse – Measure)**
**D:** Define problem
**R:** Research
**E:** Execute
**A:** Analyse
**M:** Measure
**Example:**
* Define → Declining retention
* Research → CRM + interviews
* Execute → Retention program
* Analyse → Behavior comparison
* Measure → Churn rate, usage, NPS
## **6. P-A-C-T (Problem – Approach – Compromise – Test)**
**P:** Define problem
**A:** Approach
**C:** Trade-offs
**T:** Testing/measurement
**Example:**
* Problem → Low conversion
* Approach → Improve onboarding
* Compromise → Delay feature rollout
* Test → A/B testing
## **7. C-A-R-E (Context – Action – Result – Example)**
**C:** Context
**A:** Action
**R:** Result
**E:** Example
**Example:**
* Context → Low onboarding engagement
* Action → Redesign onboarding
* Result → Higher adoption
* Example → Activation increased from 25% → 42%
## **8. R-I-S-E (Role – Input – Steps – Expectation)**
**R:** Role
**I:** Input
**S:** Steps
**E:** Expectation
**Example:**
* Role → Commercial director
* Input → Sales data
* Steps → Identify weak points
* Expectation → Growth strategy
# 🚀 **SYSTEM-LEVEL EXPANSION (ADVANCED ENGINEERING VIEW)**
Now I’ll transform these into **AI Prompt Systems** (how they actually function in real-world software engineering, AI pipelines, and SDLC).
# 🔷 **1. RTF → INPUT-OUTPUT CONTROL SYSTEM**
### 🔧 System Type:
**Instruction Mapping System**
### ⚙️ How it works:
* **Role = Context Injection Layer**
* **Task = Core Processing Instruction**
* **Format = Output Constraint Engine**
### 🧠 Internal Flow:
User Input → Role Conditioning → Task Execution → Format Structuring → Output
### 🏗 Use Cases:
* API response design
* UI content generation
* Documentation systems
### ⚠️ Strength:
High precision output formatting
### ❌ Weakness:
No deep reasoning or constraints
# 🔷 **2. SOLVE → STRATEGIC PLANNING SYSTEM**
### 🔧 System Type:
**Decision + Planning System**
### ⚙️ How it works:
* Converts vague problems into executable strategy
### 🧠 Flow:
Context → Goal → Constraints → Vision → Execution Plan
### 🏗 Use Cases:
* Product management
* Startup strategy
* System architecture planning
### ⚠️ Strength:
Handles ambiguity well
### ❌ Weakness:
Can become verbose
# 🔷 **3. TAG → ACTION EXECUTION SYSTEM**
### 🔧 System Type:
**Task Pipeline System**
### ⚙️ Flow:
Task → Action → Goal → Output
### 🏗 Use Cases:
* Automation scripts
* DevOps tasks
* Microservices instructions
### ⚠️ Strength:
Fast and efficient
### ❌ Weakness:
Lacks context depth
# 🔷 **4. RACE → CONTEXT-AWARE GENERATION SYSTEM**
### 🔧 System Type:
**Contextual AI Generation System**
### ⚙️ Flow:
Role → Action → Context → Expectation → Output
### 🏗 Use Cases:
* Personalized UX systems
* AI assistants
* CRM automation
### ⚠️ Strength:
Highly adaptive
### ❌ Weakness:
Requires detailed input
# 🔷 **5. DREAM → FULL SDLC SYSTEM**
### 🔧 System Type:
**Lifecycle Management System**
### ⚙️ Maps directly to:
* Requirements → Research → Development → Testing → Metrics
### 🧠 Flow:
Problem → Data → Implementation → Analysis → Metrics
### 🏗 Use Cases:
* Software engineering lifecycle
* Data science workflows
* AI model training pipelines
### ⚠️ Strength:
End-to-end system thinking
### ❌ Weakness:
Time-consuming
# 🔷 **6. PACT → OPTIMIZATION SYSTEM**
### 🔧 System Type:
**Trade-off Decision System**
### ⚙️ Flow:
Problem → Approach → Trade-offs → Testing
### 🏗 Use Cases:
* System optimization
* Performance tuning
* Feature prioritization
### ⚠️ Strength:
Balances constraints
### ❌ Weakness:
Requires analytical thinking
# 🔷 **7. CARE → FEEDBACK & IMPROVEMENT SYSTEM**
### 🔧 System Type:
**Evaluation + Feedback Loop System**
### ⚙️ Flow:
Context → Action → Result → Validation Example
### 🏗 Use Cases:
* UX improvement
* Machine learning evaluation
* QA systems
### ⚠️ Strength:
Great for learning loops
### ❌ Weakness:
Not ideal for planning
# 🔷 **8. RISE → STEP-BY-STEP EXECUTION SYSTEM**
### 🔧 System Type:
**Guided Instruction System**
### ⚙️ Flow:
Role → Input → Steps → Expected Output
### 🏗 Use Cases:
* Tutorials
* Learning systems
* AI copilots
### ⚠️ Strength:
Highly structured
### ❌ Weakness:
Can be rigid
# 🧠 **MASTER INTEGRATION (ADVANCED SYSTEM DESIGN)**
You can combine all frameworks into one **Unified Prompt Architecture (UPA):**
[ROLE]
[CONTEXT / SITUATION]
[TASK / PROBLEM]
[INPUT DATA]
[CONSTRAINTS]
[ACTION / APPROACH]
[STEPS / EXECUTION]
[EXPECTED OUTPUT FORMAT]
[METRICS / TESTING]
[FEEDBACK LOOP]
# 🔥 **ULTIMATE SYSTEM PROMPT (ENGINEERING-GRADE)**
Role: [Define AI role]
Context: [Full background]
Problem/Task: [What needs to be solved]
Input: [Data, constraints]
Approach: [How to solve]
Steps:
1. …
2. …
Execution Rules:
- Accuracy
- Efficiency
- Clarity
Output Format:
- Structured
- Logical
Testing:
- Validate correctness
- Measure performance
Feedback:
- Improve results iteratively
# 🎯 **KEY INSIGHT (VERY IMPORTANT)**
Each framework is not random — they map to **real systems in computing**:
| Framework | System Equivalent     |
| --------- | --------------------- |
| RTF       | Function Signature    |
| SOLVE     | System Design Process |
| TAG       | Execution Pipeline    |
| RACE      | Context Engine        |
| DREAM     | SDLC                  |
| PACT      | Optimization Model    |
| CARE      | Feedback Loop         |
| RISE      | Algorithm Steps       |

2. Which is best for fast execution?
3. Which handles constraints best?
4. Which is ideal for tutorials?

* They are **mini systems of thinking**

# 🎯 PROFESSIONAL MASTER PROMPT SYSTEM
## For Motion, 3D, Animation, Graphics, UI/UX Libraries & Frameworks

---

> **Built using RTF + SOLVE + RACE + DREAM + RISE + UPA (Unified Prompt Architecture)**
> **By a Senior 10+ Year Frontend/UI-UX/Motion Engineer | Bloom's Taxonomy L1–L6**

---

# 🔷 PART 1: UNIFIED MASTER PROMPT (COPY & USE)

```
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
🧠 ROLE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Architect with 10+ years of experience building world-class,
```

#### 1. GSAP (GreenSock Animation Platform)
> **THE GOAT OF WEB ANIMATION**

```bash
# npm
npm install gsap

# CDN
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/gsap@3.12.5/dist/gsap.min.js
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/gsap@3.12.5/dist/ScrollTrigger.min.js
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/gsap@3.12.5/dist/TextPlugin.min.js
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/gsap@3.12.5/dist/MorphSVGPlugin.min.js
```

```javascript
// ============================================================
// GSAP — COMPLETE WORKING EXAMPLE
// Senior-level implementation with timeline + ScrollTrigger
// ============================================================

// HTML Structure
/*
<div class="hero">
  <h1 class="hero__title">Motion Magic</h1>
  <p class="hero__sub">Built with GSAP</p>
  <button class="hero__cta">Get Started</button>
</div>
<div class="cards">
  <div class="card">Feature 1</div>
  <div class="card">Feature 2</div>
  <div class="card">Feature 3</div>
</div>
*/

// Register plugins
gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger, TextPlugin);

// ── HERO ENTRANCE TIMELINE ──────────────────────────────────
const heroTL = gsap.timeline({
  defaults: { 
    ease: "power3.out", 
    duration: 0.8 
  }
});

heroTL
  .from(".hero__title", { 
    y: 60, 
    opacity: 0, 
    duration: 1,
    ease: "expo.out"
  })
  .from(".hero__sub", { 
    y: 30, 
    opacity: 0 
  }, "-=0.5")
  .from(".hero__cta", { 
    scale: 0.8, 
    opacity: 0,
    ease: "back.out(1.7)" 
  }, "-=0.3");

// ── SCROLL-TRIGGERED CARD STAGGER ──────────────────────────
gsap.from(".card", {
  scrollTrigger: {
    trigger: ".cards",
    start: "top 80%",
    end: "bottom 20%",
    toggleActions: "play none none reverse",
    markers: false // set true for debugging
  },
  y: 80,
  opacity: 0,
  duration: 0.7,
  stagger: {
    amount: 0.5,
    from: "start",
    ease: "power2.out"
  }
});

// ── MAGNETIC BUTTON EFFECT ──────────────────────────────────
const btn = document.querySelector(".hero__cta");

btn.addEventListener("mousemove", (e) => {
  const rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
  const x = e.clientX - rect.left - rect.width / 2;
  const y = e.clientY - rect.top - rect.height / 2;
  
  gsap.to(btn, {
    x: x * 0.3,
    y: y * 0.3,
    duration: 0.3,
    ease: "power2.out"
  });
});

btn.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => {
  gsap.to(btn, { 
    x: 0, 
    y: 0, 
    duration: 0.5, 
    ease: "elastic.out(1, 0.3)" 
  });
});

// ── TEXT SCRAMBLE EFFECT ────────────────────────────────────
gsap.to(".hero__title", {
  duration: 2,
  text: {
    value: "Motion Magic",
    delimiter: "",
    speed: 0.5
  },
  ease: "none"
});
```

| Property | Value |
|----------|-------|
| ⭐ Rating | 5/5 |
| 📦 Bundle | 23kb gzipped |
| 📈 Learning | Medium |
| 🎯 Best For | Everything — timeline, scroll, SVG, text |
| 🏆 Rank | #1 Most Powerful |

#### 2. Framer Motion
> **BEST FOR REACT ECOSYSTEM**

```bash
# npm
npm install framer-motion

# CDN (ESM)
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/framer-motion@11.0.0/dist/framer-motion.js
```

```jsx
// ============================================================
// FRAMER MOTION — COMPLETE REACT EXAMPLE
// Production-grade with variants, gestures, layout animation
// ============================================================

import { 
  motion, 
  AnimatePresence, 
  useScroll, 
  useTransform,
  useSpring,
  useMotionValue
} from "framer-motion";
import { useState } from "react";

// ── ANIMATION VARIANTS (Design System Approach) ─────────────
const variants = {
  container: {
    hidden: { opacity: 0 },
    visible: {
      opacity: 1,
      transition: {
        staggerChildren: 0.1,
        delayChildren: 0.2
      }
    }
  },
  item: {
    hidden: { y: 40, opacity: 0 },
    visible: {
      y: 0,
      opacity: 1,
      transition: {
        type: "spring",
        stiffness: 300,
        damping: 24
      }
    }
  },
  card: {
    rest: { scale: 1, boxShadow: "0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)" },
    hover: { 
      scale: 1.04, 
      boxShadow: "0 16px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.2)",
      transition: { type: "spring", stiffness: 400, damping: 17 }
    },
    tap: { scale: 0.97 }
  }
};

// ── SCROLL PARALLAX HOOK ────────────────────────────────────
function ParallaxSection() {
  const { scrollYProgress } = useScroll();
  const y = useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 1], [0, -200]);
  const opacity = useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 0.5], [1, 0]);
  const scale = useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 0.5], [1, 1.2]);

  return (
    <motion.div style={{ y, opacity, scale }}>
      <h1>Scroll to Animate</h1>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

// ── ANIMATED CARD COMPONENT ─────────────────────────────────
function AnimatedCard({ title, index }) {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);

  return (
    <motion.div
      variants={variants.card}
      initial="rest"
      whileHover="hover"
      whileTap="tap"
      layout // automatic layout animation
      onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}
      style={{
        borderRadius: 16,
        padding: 24,
        background: "white",
        cursor: "pointer"
      }}
    >
      <motion.h3 layoutId={`title-${index}`}>
        {title}
      </motion.h3>
      
      <AnimatePresence>
        {isOpen && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ height: "auto", opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: "easeInOut" }}
          >
            <p>Expanded content with smooth height animation</p>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

// ── MAIN COMPONENT ──────────────────────────────────────────
export function AnimatedPage() {
  const cards = ["Design", "Motion", "Code"];

  return (
    <motion.div
      variants={variants.container}
      initial="hidden"
      animate="visible"
    >
      {cards.map((card, i) => (
        <motion.div key={card} variants={variants.item}>
          <AnimatedCard title={card} index={i} />
        </motion.div>
      ))}
    </motion.div>
  );
}
```

#### 3. Anime.js
> **LIGHTWEIGHT + POWERFUL + CSS PROPERTIES**

```bash
# npm
npm install animejs

# CDN
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/animejs@3.2.2/lib/anime.min.js
```

```javascript
// ============================================================
// ANIME.JS — COMPLETE WORKING EXAMPLE
// SVG morphing, stagger, timeline, CSS vars
// ============================================================

import anime from 'animejs/lib/anime.es.js';

// ── STAGGERED ENTRANCE ──────────────────────────────────────
anime({
  targets: '.element',
  translateY: [-40, 0],
  opacity: [0, 1],
  duration: 800,
  delay: anime.stagger(100, { start: 300 }),
  easing: 'easeOutExpo'
});

// ── SVG PATH MORPHING ───────────────────────────────────────
anime({
  targets: '#morph-shape',
  d: [
    { value: 'M0,0 C150,0 150,150 300,150' },
    { value: 'M0,150 C150,150 150,0 300,0' }
  ],
  duration: 2000,
  loop: true,
  direction: 'alternate',
  easing: 'easeInOutSine'
});

// ── CSS VARIABLE ANIMATION ──────────────────────────────────
anime({
  targets: document.documentElement,
  '--primary-hue': [200, 280],
  duration: 3000,
  loop: true,
  direction: 'alternate',
  easing: 'easeInOutQuad'
});

// ── TIMELINE SEQUENCE ───────────────────────────────────────
const tl = anime.timeline({
  easing: 'easeOutExpo',
  duration: 750
});

tl
  .add({ targets: '.logo', scale: [0, 1], opacity: [0, 1] })
  .add({ targets: '.nav', translateY: [-20, 0], opacity: [0, 1] }, '-=400')
  .add({ targets: '.hero', translateX: [-60, 0], opacity: [0, 1] }, '-=300')
  .add({ targets: '.cta', scale: [0.5, 1], opacity: [0, 1] }, '-=200');

// ── COUNTER ANIMATION ───────────────────────────────────────
anime({
  targets: '.counter',
  innerHTML: [0, 1500],
  round: 1,
  duration: 2000,
  easing: 'easeInOutExpo'
});
```

#### 4. Motion One
> **NATIVE WEB ANIMATIONS API — LIGHTEST OPTION**

```bash
# npm
npm install motion

# CDN
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/motion@10.18.0/dist/motion.js
```

```javascript
// ============================================================
// MOTION ONE — WEB ANIMATIONS API WRAPPER
// Native performance, smallest bundle
// ============================================================

import { animate, scroll, inView, stagger } from "motion";

// ── BASIC ANIMATION ─────────────────────────────────────────
animate(
  ".box",
  { 
    x: [0, 100], 
    rotate: [0, 360],
    borderRadius: ["0%", "50%"]
  },
  { 
    duration: 1, 
    easing: "ease-in-out",
    repeat: Infinity,
    direction: "alternate"
  }
);

// ── SCROLL-LINKED ANIMATION ─────────────────────────────────
scroll(
  animate(".progress-bar", { scaleX: [0, 1] }),
  { target: document.body }
);

// ── IN-VIEW TRIGGER ─────────────────────────────────────────
inView(".card", ({ target }) => {
  animate(target, 
    { opacity: [0, 1], y: [30, 0] },
    { duration: 0.6, easing: [0.17, 0.55, 0.55, 1] }
  );
});

// ── STAGGER ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
animate(
  ".list-item",
  { opacity: [0, 1], x: [-20, 0] },
  { delay: stagger(0.05), duration: 0.4 }
);
```

#### 1. Three.js
> **INDUSTRY STANDARD 3D ON THE WEB**

```bash
# npm
npm install three

# CDN
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.169.0/build/three.module.js
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.169.0/build/three.min.js

# Addons
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.169.0/examples/jsm/controls/OrbitControls.js
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.169.0/examples/jsm/loaders/GLTFLoader.js
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.169.0/examples/jsm/postprocessing/EffectComposer.js
```

```javascript
// ============================================================
// THREE.JS — PRODUCTION-GRADE 3D SCENE
// Geometry + Material + Lighting + Animation + Post-processing
// ============================================================

import * as THREE from 'three';
import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';
import { GLTFLoader } from 'three/addons/loaders/GLTFLoader.js';
import { EffectComposer } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/EffectComposer.js';
import { RenderPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/RenderPass.js';
import { UnrealBloomPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/UnrealBloomPass.js';

// ── SCENE SETUP ─────────────────────────────────────────────
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x0a0a0a);
scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x0a0a0a, 0.02);

// ── CAMERA ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(
  75,                                    // FOV
  window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, // Aspect
  0.1,                                   // Near
  1000                                   // Far
);
camera.position.set(0, 2, 8);

// ── RENDERER (Production Settings) ──────────────────────────
const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ 
  antialias: true,
  alpha: true,
  powerPreference: "high-performance"
});
renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.2;
renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

// ── LIGHTING SYSTEM ─────────────────────────────────────────
const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.4);
scene.add(ambientLight);

const directionalLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 2);
directionalLight.position.set(5, 10, 5);
directionalLight.castShadow = true;
directionalLight.shadow.mapSize.setScalar(2048);
scene.add(directionalLight);

const pointLight1 = new THREE.PointLight(0x4080ff, 3, 20);
pointLight1.position.set(-5, 3, 0);
scene.add(pointLight1);

const pointLight2 = new THREE.PointLight(0xff4060, 3, 20);
pointLight2.position.set(5, 3, 0);
scene.add(pointLight2);

// ── GEOMETRY + MATERIAL ─────────────────────────────────────
const geometry = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1.5, 4);
const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
  color: 0x2080ff,
  metalness: 0.9,
  roughness: 0.1,
  wireframe: false,
  envMapIntensity: 1
});

const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
mesh.castShadow = true;
scene.add(mesh);

// ── PARTICLE SYSTEM ─────────────────────────────────────────
const particleCount = 3000;
const positions = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);
const colors = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);

for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i++) {
  positions[i * 3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20;
  positions[i * 3 + 1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20;
  positions[i * 3 + 2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20;
  
  colors[i * 3] = Math.random();
  colors[i * 3 + 1] = Math.random() * 0.5 + 0.5;
  colors[i * 3 + 2] = 1;
}

const particleGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
particleGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
particleGeo.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors, 3));

const particleMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
  size: 0.05, 
  vertexColors: true,
  transparent: true,
  opacity: 0.8,
  sizeAttenuation: true
});

scene.add(new THREE.Points(particleGeo, particleMat));

// ── POST PROCESSING — BLOOM ──────────────────────────────────
const composer = new EffectComposer(renderer);
composer.addPass(new RenderPass(scene, camera));
composer.addPass(new UnrealBloomPass(
  new THREE.Vector2(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight),
  0.5,  // strength
  0.4,  // radius
  0.85  // threshold
));

// ── CONTROLS ────────────────────────────────────────────────
const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
controls.enableDamping = true;
controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
controls.autoRotate = true;
controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.5;

// ── ANIMATION LOOP ──────────────────────────────────────────
const clock = new THREE.Clock();

function animate() {
  requestAnimationFrame(animate);
  
  const elapsed = clock.getElapsedTime();
  
  // Geometry animation
  mesh.rotation.x = elapsed * 0.3;
  mesh.rotation.y = elapsed * 0.5;
  mesh.position.y = Math.sin(elapsed * 0.8) * 0.3;
  
  // Pulsing scale
  const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(elapsed * 2) * 0.05;
  mesh.scale.setScalar(pulse);
  
  // Light animation
  pointLight1.position.x = Math.sin(elapsed) * 5;
  pointLight2.position.x = Math.cos(elapsed) * 5;
  
  controls.update();
  composer.render();
}

animate();

// ── RESPONSIVE HANDLER ───────────────────────────────────────
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
  camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
  camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
  composer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
});
```

#### 2. React Three Fiber (R3F)
> **THREE.JS FOR REACT — DECLARATIVE 3D**

```bash
# npm
npm install @react-three/fiber @react-three/drei three

# CDN — Use as npm package (no CDN for R3F)
```

```jsx
// ============================================================
// REACT THREE FIBER — COMPLETE 3D SCENE IN REACT
// With Drei helpers, physics, postprocessing
// ============================================================

import { Canvas, useFrame, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { 
  OrbitControls, 
  Environment, 
  Float,
  MeshDistortMaterial,
  Sparkles,
  Stars,
  Text3D,
  ContactShadows,
  useGLTF,
  Html
} from '@react-three/drei';
import { useRef, useState, Suspense } from 'react';
import { EffectComposer, Bloom, ChromaticAberration } from '@react-three/postprocessing';

// ── ANIMATED MESH ───────────────────────────────────────────
function AnimatedSphere({ position = [0, 0, 0] }) {
  const ref = useRef();
  const [hovered, setHovered] = useState(false);

  useFrame((state) => {
    const t = state.clock.elapsedTime;
    ref.current.rotation.x = t * 0.3;
    ref.current.rotation.y = t * 0.5;
    ref.current.position.y = Math.sin(t * 0.8) * 0.3;
  });

  return (
    <Float speed={2} rotationIntensity={0.5} floatIntensity={0.5}>
      <mesh
        ref={ref}
        position={position}
        scale={hovered ? 1.1 : 1}
        onPointerOver={() => setHovered(true)}
        onPointerOut={() => setHovered(false)}
      >
        <icosahedronGeometry args={[1.5, 4]} />
        <MeshDistortMaterial
          color={hovered ? "#ff4060" : "#2080ff"}
          metalness={0.9}
          roughness={0.1}
          distort={0.3}
          speed={2}
        />
      </mesh>
    </Float>
  );
}

// ── MAIN SCENE ──────────────────────────────────────────────
export function Scene3D() {
  return (
    <Canvas
      camera={{ position: [0, 2, 8], fov: 75 }}
      gl={{ 
        antialias: true,
        powerPreference: "high-performance",
        toneMapping: THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping
      }}
      shadows
      style={{ width: '100vw', height: '100vh' }}
    >
      <Suspense fallback={null}>
        {/* Environment */}
        <Environment preset="city" />
        <Stars radius={100} depth={50} count={5000} factor={4} />
        
        {/* Lighting */}
        <ambientLight intensity={0.4} />
        <directionalLight 
          position={[5, 10, 5]} 
          intensity={2} 
          castShadow 
          shadow-mapSize={[2048, 2048]}
        />
        <pointLight position={[-5, 3, 0]} color="#4080ff" intensity={3} />
        <pointLight position={[5, 3, 0]} color="#ff4060" intensity={3} />
        
        {/* Geometry */}
        <AnimatedSphere position={[0, 0, 0]} />
        
        {/* Particles */}
        <Sparkles 
          count={200} 
          scale={12} 
          size={1.5} 
          speed={0.4}
          color="#4080ff"
        />
        
        {/* Ground shadow */}
        <ContactShadows
          position={[0, -2.5, 0]}
          opacity={0.6}
          scale={12}
          blur={2.5}
        />
        
        {/* Controls */}
        <OrbitControls 
          enableDamping 
          autoRotate 
          autoRotateSpeed={0.5}
          minDistance={3}
          maxDistance={15}
        />
        
        {/* Post Processing */}
        <EffectComposer>
          <Bloom 
            intensity={0.5} 
            luminanceThreshold={0.8}
            luminanceSmoothing={0.9}
          />
          <ChromaticAberration offset={[0.001, 0.001]} />
        </EffectComposer>
      </Suspense>
    </Canvas>
  );
}
```

```jsx
// ============================================================
// SHADCN/UI — BEST PRACTICE IMPLEMENTATION
// Production-ready component patterns
// ============================================================

// components/ui/animated-card.tsx
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface CardProps {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  className?: string;
  delay?: number;
}

export function AnimatedCard({ 
  title, 
  description, 
  className,
  delay = 0 
}: CardProps) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 24 }}
      whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      viewport={{ once: true, margin: "-50px" }}
      transition={{ 
        duration: 0.5, 
        delay,
        ease: [0.21, 0.45, 0.27, 0.9]
      }}
      whileHover={{ y: -4 }}
      className={cn(
        "group relative overflow-hidden rounded-xl border bg-card p-6",
        "shadow-sm hover:shadow-xl transition-shadow duration-300",
        className
      )}
    >
      {/* Gradient overlay on hover */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-primary/5 to-transparent opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300" />
      
      <h3 className="font-semibold text-lg text-foreground mb-2">{title}</h3>
      <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm leading-relaxed">
        {description}
      </p>
    </motion.div>
  );
}
```

```css
/* ============================================================
   CSS-ONLY ANIMATION SYSTEM — PRODUCTION GRADE
   Senior-level CSS architecture with custom properties
   ============================================================ */

/* ── CSS CUSTOM PROPERTIES DESIGN TOKENS ─────────────────── */
:root {
  /* Timing functions */
  --ease-out-expo: cubic-bezier(0.19, 1, 0.22, 1);
  --ease-out-back: cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1);
  --ease-in-out-quart: cubic-bezier(0.76, 0, 0.24, 1);
  --ease-spring: cubic-bezier(0.68, -0.6, 0.32, 1.6);
  
  /* Duration scale */
  --duration-fast: 150ms;
  --duration-base: 300ms;
  --duration-slow: 500ms;
  --duration-slower: 800ms;
  --duration-slowest: 1200ms;
  
  /* Motion preferences */
  --motion-scale: 1;
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  :root { --motion-scale: 0; }
  *, *::before, *::after {
    animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
  }
}

/* ── KEYFRAME LIBRARY ────────────────────────────────────── */

@keyframes fadeUp {
  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(calc(24px * var(--motion-scale))); }
  to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
}

@keyframes fadeDown {
  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(calc(-24px * var(--motion-scale))); }
  to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
}

@keyframes scaleIn {
  from { opacity: 0; transform: scale(calc(0.92 * var(--motion-scale, 0.92))); }
  to { opacity: 1; transform: scale(1); }
}

@keyframes slideRight {
  from { transform: translateX(-100%); }
  to { transform: translateX(0); }
}

@keyframes rotateIn {
  from { opacity: 0; transform: rotate(-10deg) scale(0.9); }
  to { opacity: 1; transform: rotate(0) scale(1); }
}

@keyframes shimmer {
  from { background-position: -200% center; }
  to { background-position: 200% center; }
}

@keyframes pulse {
  0%, 100% { transform: scale(1); opacity: 1; }
  50% { transform: scale(1.05); opacity: 0.8; }
}

@keyframes spin {
  to { transform: rotate(360deg); }
}

@keyframes bounce {
  0%, 100% { transform: translateY(0); animation-timing-function: ease-in; }
  50% { transform: translateY(-20px); animation-timing-function: ease-out; }
}

@keyframes float {
  0%, 100% { transform: translateY(0px) rotate(0deg); }
  33% { transform: translateY(-10px) rotate(1deg); }
  66% { transform: translateY(-5px) rotate(-1deg); }
}

@keyframes gradient-shift {
  0% { background-position: 0% 50%; }
  50% { background-position: 100% 50%; }
  100% { background-position: 0% 50%; }
}

@keyframes typewriter {
  from { width: 0; }
  to { width: 100%; }
}

@keyframes blink {
  0%, 100% { border-color: transparent; }
  50% { border-color: currentColor; }
}

/* ── UTILITY ANIMATION CLASSES ───────────────────────────── */

.animate-fade-up {
  animation: fadeUp var(--duration-slow) var(--ease-out-expo) both;
}

.animate-fade-down {
  animation: fadeDown var(--duration-slow) var(--ease-out-expo) both;
}

}

  animation: float 4s ease-in-out infinite;
}

}

}

/* ── STAGGER DELAYS ─────────────────────────────────────────*/

.stagger-1 { animation-delay: 100ms; }
.stagger-2 { animation-delay: 200ms; }
.stagger-3 { animation-delay: 300ms; }

  background-size: 200% auto;
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  background-clip: text;

/* ── SHIMMER LOADING ────────────────────────────────────── */

    90deg,
```

```javascript
// ============================================================
// AUTO-ANIMATE — ZERO CONFIG LIST ANIMATIONS
// Add animation to any element automatically
// ============================================================

import autoAnimate from '@formkit/auto-animate';
import { useAutoAnimate } from '@formkit/auto-animate/react';

// ── VANILLA JS ───────────────────────────────────────────────
const list = document.querySelector('#list');
autoAnimate(list, {
  duration: 250,
  easing: 'ease-in-out'
});

// Now any DOM changes to #list are animated automatically
list.appendChild(newItem);      // → slides in
list.removeChild(oldItem);      // → fades out
list.insertBefore(item, other); // → smooth reorder

// ── REACT HOOK ───────────────────────────────────────────────
function AnimatedList({ items }) {
  const [parent] = useAutoAnimate({
    duration: 300,
    easing: 'ease-out'
  });

  return (
    <ul ref={parent}>
      {items.map(item => (
        <li key={item.id}>{item.name}</li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}

// ============================================================
// CUSTOM PAGE TRANSITION SYSTEM
// Works with React Router / Next.js
// ============================================================

// transitions.js
export const pageVariants = {
  // Slide transition
  slide: {
    initial: { x: '100%', opacity: 0 },
    animate: { x: 0, opacity: 1 },
    exit: { x: '-100%', opacity: 0 },
    transition: { type: 'tween', duration: 0.4, ease: 'easeInOut' }
  },
  
  // Fade + Scale
  fade: {
    initial: { opacity: 0, scale: 0.96 },
    animate: { opacity: 1, scale: 1 },
    exit: { opacity: 0, scale: 1.04 },
    transition: { duration: 0.3, ease: 'easeOut' }
  },
  
  // Curtain reveal
  curtain: {
    initial: { clipPath: 'inset(0 100% 0 0)' },
    animate: { clipPath: 'inset(0 0% 0 0)' },
    exit: { clipPath: 'inset(0 0 0 100%)' },
    transition: { duration: 0.5, ease: 'easeInOut' }
  },
  
  // Flip
  flip: {
    initial: { rotateY: 90, opacity: 0 },
    animate: { rotateY: 0, opacity: 1 },
    exit: { rotateY: -90, opacity: 0 },
    transition: { duration: 0.4 }
  }
};

// PageWrapper.jsx
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';

export function PageWrapper({ children, transition = 'fade' }) {
  const location = useLocation();
  const variant = pageVariants[transition];
  
  return (
    <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
      <motion.div
        key={location.pathname}
        initial={variant.initial}
        animate={variant.animate}
        exit={variant.exit}
        transition={variant.transition}
        style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }}
      >
        {children}
      </motion.div>
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}
```

```javascript
// ============================================================
// TSPARTICLES — COMPLETE CONFIGURATION SYSTEM
// Production-grade particle effects
// ============================================================

import { tsParticles } from "@tsparticles/engine";
import { loadSlim } from "@tsparticles/slim";

await loadSlim(tsParticles);

// ── STARFIELD EFFECT ────────────────────────────────────────
await tsParticles.load({
  id: "particles",
  options: {
    fullScreen: { enable: true, zIndex: -1 },
    background: { color: { value: "#0a0a0f" } },
    fpsLimit: 120,
    particles: {
      number: { value: 160, density: { enable: true, area: 800 } },
      color: { value: ["#4080ff", "#ff4060", "#40ffaa"] },
      shape: { type: "circle" },
      opacity: {
        value: { min: 0.1, max: 0.8 },
        animation: {
          enable: true,
          speed: 1,
          minimumValue: 0.1,
          sync: false
        }
      },
      size: {
        value: { min: 0.5, max: 2.5 },
        animation: {
          enable: true,
          speed: 2,
          minimumValue: 0.3
        }
      },
      links: {
        enable: true,
        distance: 120,
        color: "#4080ff",
        opacity: 0.15,
        width: 1,
        triangles: { enable: false }
      },
      move: {
        enable: true,
        speed: 0.5,
        direction: "none",
        random: true,
        outModes: "bounce",
        attract: { enable: false }
      }
    },
    interactivity: {
      detectsOn: "window",
      events: {
        onHover: {
          enable: true,
          mode: ["grab", "bubble"]
        },
        onClick: {
          enable: true,
          mode: "repulse"
        }
      },
      modes: {
        grab: { distance: 200, links: { opacity: 0.5 } },
        bubble: { distance: 200, size: 5, opacity: 0.8 },
        repulse: { distance: 150, duration: 0.4 }
      }
    },
    detectRetina: true
  }
});

// ── CONFETTI CELEBRATION ────────────────────────────────────
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';

function celebrate() {
  // Burst from both sides
  confetti({
    particleCount: 100,
    spread: 70,
    origin: { x: 0.2, y: 0.6 },
    colors: ['#4080ff', '#ff4060', '#40ffaa', '#ffaa00']
  });
  
  setTimeout(() => {
    confetti({
      particleCount: 100,
      spread: 70,
      origin: { x: 0.8, y: 0.6 },
      colors: ['#4080ff', '#ff4060', '#40ffaa', '#ffaa00']
    });
  }, 200);
  
  // Continuous falling
  const end = Date.now() + 3000;
  const interval = setInterval(() => {
    if (Date.now() > end) { clearInterval(interval); return; }
    confetti({
      particleCount: 2,
      angle: 60,
      spread: 55,
      origin: { x: 0 },
      ticks: 200
    });
    confetti({
      particleCount: 2,
      angle: 120,
      spread: 55,
      origin: { x: 1 },
      ticks: 200
    });
  }, 10);
}

// ── VANTA ANIMATED BACKGROUNDS ──────────────────────────────
import VANTA from 'vanta/dist/vanta.waves.min';
import * as THREE from 'three';

const effect = VANTA.WAVES({
  el: "#vanta-bg",
  THREE,
  color: 0x1a1a4e,
  shininess: 50,
  waveHeight: 15,
  waveSpeed: 0.8,
  zoom: 0.9
});

// Cleanup
window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => effect.destroy());
```

## 📜 CATEGORY 7: SCROLL EFFECTS

```bash

https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/aos@2.3.4/dist/aos.js
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/aos@2.3.4/dist/aos.css

# ScrollReveal
npm install scrollreveal
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/scrollreveal@4.0.9/dist/scrollreveal.min.js

# Intersection Observer (Native — Zero deps)
# Built into all modern browsers - no install needed
```

```javascript
// ============================================================
// COMPLETE SCROLL ANIMATION SYSTEM
// Lenis smooth scroll + GSAP ScrollTrigger + Intersection Observer
// ============================================================

import Lenis from 'lenis';
import gsap from 'gsap';
import { ScrollTrigger } from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger';

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

// ── LENIS SMOOTH SCROLL SETUP ────────────────────────────────
const lenis = new Lenis({
  duration: 1.2,
  easing: (t) => Math.min(1, 1.001 - Math.pow(2, -10 * t)),
  direction: 'vertical',
  gestureDirection: 'vertical',
  smooth: true,
  smoothTouch: false,
  touchMultiplier: 2,
  infinite: false
});

// Connect Lenis to GSAP ticker
lenis.on('scroll', ScrollTrigger.update);

gsap.ticker.add((time) => {
  lenis.raf(time * 1000);
});

gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0);

// ── PARALLAX HERO ───────────────────────────────────────────
gsap.to(".hero__bg", {
  yPercent: -30,
  ease: "none",
  scrollTrigger: {
    trigger: ".hero",
    start: "top top",
    end: "bottom top",
    scrub: true
  }
});

// ── HORIZONTAL SCROLL SECTION ────────────────────────────────
const sections = gsap.utils.toArray(".panel");

gsap.to(sections, {
  xPercent: -100 * (sections.length - 1),
  ease: "none",
  scrollTrigger: {
    trigger: ".horizontal-scroll",
    pin: true,
    scrub: 1,
    snap: 1 / (sections.length - 1),
    end: () => "+=" + document.querySelector(".horizontal-scroll").offsetWidth
  }
});

// ── STAGGERED REVEAL ON SCROLL ───────────────────────────────
gsap.utils.toArray(".reveal-item").forEach((el, i) => {
  gsap.from(el, {
    scrollTrigger: {
      trigger: el,
      start: "top 85%",
      toggleActions: "play none none reverse"
    },
    y: 60,
    opacity: 0,
    duration: 0.8,
    delay: (i % 3) * 0.1, // stagger within groups
    ease: "power3.out"
  });
});

// ── TEXT REVEAL (WORD BY WORD) ───────────────────────────────
function splitTextIntoWords(selector) {
  document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(el => {
    const words = el.innerText.split(' ');
    el.innerHTML = words.map(word => 
      `<span class="word"><span class="word__inner">${word}</span></span>`
    ).join(' ');
  });
}

splitTextIntoWords('.reveal-text');

gsap.utils.toArray('.reveal-text').forEach(text => {
  gsap.from(text.querySelectorAll('.word__inner'), {
    scrollTrigger: {
      trigger: text,
      start: "top 80%"
    },
    y: '110%',
    duration: 0.6,
    stagger: 0.04,
    ease: "power3.out"
  });
});

// ── COUNTER ANIMATION ────────────────────────────────────────
gsap.utils.toArray('.counter').forEach(counter => {
  const target = parseInt(counter.getAttribute('data-target'));
  
  gsap.to({ val: 0 }, {
    val: target,
    duration: 2,
    ease: "power2.out",
    scrollTrigger: {
      trigger: counter,
      start: "top 80%",
      once: true
    },
    onUpdate: function() {
      counter.innerText = Math.ceil(this.targets()[0].val).toLocaleString();
    }
  });
});

// ── NATIVE INTERSECTION OBSERVER (ZERO DEPS) ─────────────────
const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
  (entries) => {
    entries.forEach(entry => {
      if (entry.isIntersecting) {
        entry.target.classList.add('in-view');
        observer.unobserve(entry.target); // once
      }
    });
  },
  { 
    threshold: 0.1,
    rootMargin: '0px 0px -50px 0px'
  }
);

document.querySelectorAll('[data-animate]').forEach(el => {
  observer.observe(el);
});
```

```javascript
// ============================================================

// design-tokens.js
export const tokens = {
  // ── BASE PALETTE (OKLCH - perceptually uniform) ───────────
  colors: {

    primary: {
      50:  'oklch(97% 0.02 250)',
      100: 'oklch(93% 0.05 250)',

      300: 'oklch(79% 0.13 250)',

      950: 'oklch(20% 0.05 250)',
    },
    
    // Semantic
    success: 'oklch(60% 0.18 155)',
    warning: 'oklch(75% 0.18 80)',
    error: 'oklch(58% 0.22 25)',
    info: 'oklch(65% 0.18 230)',
  },
  
  // ── TYPOGRAPHY SCALE (Perfect Fourth - 1.333 ratio) ──────
  typography: {
    fontFamily: {
      sans: "'Inter', 'Plus Jakarta Sans', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif",
      mono: "'Geist Mono', 'JetBrains Mono', 'Fira Code', monospace",
      display: "'Space Grotesk', 'Syne', sans-serif",
    },
    fontSize: {
      xs:   ['12px', { lineHeight: '16px', letterSpacing: '0.04em' }],
      sm:   ['14px', { lineHeight: '20px', letterSpacing: '0.02em' }],
      base: ['16px', { lineHeight: '24px', letterSpacing: '0em' }],
      lg:   ['18px', { lineHeight: '28px', letterSpacing: '-0.01em' }],
      xl:   ['20px', { lineHeight: '28px', letterSpacing: '-0.01em' }],
      '2xl':['24px', { lineHeight: '32px', letterSpacing: '-0.02em' }],
      '3xl':['30px', { lineHeight: '36px', letterSpacing: '-0.02em' }],
      '4xl':['36px', { lineHeight: '40px', letterSpacing: '-0.03em' }],
      '5xl':['48px', { lineHeight: '1',   letterSpacing: '-0.03em' }],
      '6xl':['60px', { lineHeight: '1',   letterSpacing: '-0.04em' }],
      '7xl':['72px', { lineHeight: '1',   letterSpacing: '-0.04em' }],
      '8xl':['96px', { lineHeight: '1',   letterSpacing: '-0.05em' }],
    },
    fontWeight: {
      thin: 100, light: 300, normal: 400,
      medium: 500, semibold: 600, bold: 700,
      extrabold: 800, black: 900
    }
  }
};

// ── CSS VARIABLES INJECTION ──────────────────────────────────
function injectDesignTokens() {
  const root = document.documentElement;
  
  // Light theme
  const lightTokens = {
    '--bg-primary': 'oklch(99% 0.005 250)',
    '--bg-secondary': 'oklch(96% 0.01 250)',
    '--bg-surface': 'oklch(100% 0 0)',
    '--text-primary': 'oklch(15% 0.02 250)',
    '--text-secondary': 'oklch(45% 0.02 250)',
    '--text-tertiary': 'oklch(65% 0.015 250)',
    '--border': 'oklch(88% 0.01 250)',
    '--shadow': '0 4px 16px oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.08)',
  };
  
  Object.entries(lightTokens).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    root.style.setProperty(key, value);
  });
}

// ── CHROMA.JS COLOR PALETTE GENERATOR ────────────────────────
import chroma from 'chroma-js';

function generatePalette(baseColor, name = 'brand') {
  const scale = chroma.scale([
    chroma(baseColor).brighten(3),
    baseColor,
    chroma(baseColor).darken(3)
  ]).mode('oklch').colors(11);
  
  const palette = {};
  const steps = [50, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600, 700, 800, 900, 950];
  
  scale.forEach((color, i) => {
    palette[`${name}-${steps[i]}`] = color;
  });
  
  return palette;
}

const brandPalette = generatePalette('#2080ff');
const accentPalette = generatePalette('#ff4060');
```

```markdown
# DESIGN SYSTEM — [PROJECT NAME]

## Tech Stack
- Framework: Next.js 14 + TypeScript
- Styling: Tailwind CSS + shadcn/ui
- Animation: GSAP + Framer Motion
- 3D: React Three Fiber + Drei
- Scroll: Lenis + GSAP ScrollTrigger
- Icons: Lucide React + Phosphor Icons

## Color Palette (OKLCH)
- Primary:    oklch(60% 0.20 250)  → Blue actions
- Secondary:  oklch(60% 0.20 320)  → Purple accents
- Accent:     oklch(60% 0.22 25)   → Coral highlights
- Background: oklch(99% 0.005 250) → Near-white canvas
- Surface:    oklch(100% 0 0)      → Pure white cards
- Border:     oklch(88% 0.01 250)  → Subtle dividers
- Text:       oklch(15% 0.02 250)  → Near-black body
- Muted:      oklch(45% 0.02 250)  → Gray secondary

## Typography
- Display: Space Grotesk (headings, hero text)
- Body: Inter (body, UI, captions)
- Mono: Geist Mono (code, data)
- Scale: 12/14/16/18/20/24/30/36/48/60/72/96px
- Weight system: 300/400/500/600/700/800

## Spacing (4px base)
4 / 8 / 12 / 16 / 20 / 24 / 32 / 40 / 48 / 64 / 80 / 96px

## Border Radius
- xs: 4px (tags, badges)
- sm: 8px (inputs, chips)
- md: 12px (buttons, cards)
- lg: 16px (modals, panels)
- xl: 24px (sheets, large cards)
- full: 9999px (avatars, pills)

## Animation Tokens
- Fast: 150ms
- Base: 300ms
- Slow: 500ms
- Slower: 800ms
- Ease: cubic-bezier(0.19, 1, 0.22, 1) — expo out
- Spring: type: spring, stiffness: 300, damping: 24

## Shadows
- sm: 0 1px 3px oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.06)
- md: 0 4px 16px oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.08)
- lg: 0 8px 32px oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.12)
- xl: 0 16px 64px oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.16)

## Rules
1. OKLCH colors only — no hex/rgb
2. Spacing scale only — no arbitrary values
3. All interactions animate at 60fps minimum
4. Reduce motion respected always
5. WCAG AA contrast minimum
6. Consistent border-radius per component type
7. Generous whitespace — less is more
```

# 🌟 PART 1: FOUNDATION — VISION, MISSION & PURPOSE

---

## 🔷 THE HOLY TRINITY OF COMPANY DNA

```
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║              VISION + MISSION + PURPOSE FRAMEWORK               ║
║         The 3 Pillars Every Great Company Is Built On           ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
UNDERSTANDING THE DIFFERENCE
(Most founders confuse these — this costs them everything)
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

PURPOSE  = The NORTH STAR — The deepest WHY you exist
           "We exist because the world needs [CHANGE]"
           Timeless. Never changes. Emotional. Inspiring.
           Example: Patagonia → "Save our home planet"
           Example: Tesla → "Accelerate sustainable energy"
           Example: Apple → "Think different. Challenge status quo"

VISION   = The DESTINATION — Where you're going in 10-25 years
           "We will create a world where [TRANSFORMATION]"
           Ambitious. Specific. Time-bound. Measurable direction.
           Example: Microsoft (1980) → "A computer on every desk"
           Example: Google → "Organize the world's information"
           Example: Amazon → "Most customer-centric company on Earth"

MISSION  = The DAILY VEHICLE — What you do every day to get there
           "We [ACTION] for [AUDIENCE] by [HOW]"
           Operational. Present tense. Focused. Executable.
           Example: Nike → "Bring inspiration to every athlete"
           Example: LinkedIn → "Connect professionals worldwide"
           Example: Airbnb → "Belong anywhere"

════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
THE PURPOSE DISCOVERY FRAMEWORK
(Use the 5 WHYs Method — Toyota's Sakichi Toyoda)
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Start with your business idea and ask WHY 5 times:

Example: Building a project management tool
WHY 1: "To help people manage projects"
WHY 2: "Because projects fail and people lose money"
WHY 3: "Because teams are disorganized and miscommunicated"
WHY 4: "Because they lack simple, beautiful systems"
WHY 5: "Because great work deserves great tools — and 
         most people are working harder, not smarter"

PURPOSE FOUND: 
"We exist because every person deserves tools that make 
 their work feel effortless, meaningful, and powerful."

════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
THE VISION BUILDING FRAMEWORK
(Think 10 years forward — what does the world look like?)
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

VISION FORMULA:
"By [YEAR], [COMPANY NAME] will be the world's [SUPERLATIVE]
 [CATEGORY] that [SPECIFIC TRANSFORMATION] for [SCALE OF IMPACT],
 changing the way [AUDIENCE] [BEHAVIOR/OUTCOME] forever."

VISION LEVELS:
Level 1 (Small Vision):  "Be the best [X] in our city"
Level 2 (Medium Vision): "Be the leading [X] in our country"
Level 3 (BIG Vision):    "Redefine how the world does [X]"
Level 4 (BHAG Vision):   "Make [X] obsolete / Create new category"

BHAG = Big Hairy Audacious Goal (Jim Collins)
Examples of BHAGs:
- SpaceX: "Make humanity multi-planetary"
- Khan Academy: "Free world-class education for everyone"
- Stripe: "Increase the GDP of the internet"
- OpenAI: "Ensure AI benefits all of humanity"

YOUR BHAG TEMPLATE:
"[COMPANY] will [BOLD VERB] [MASSIVE TRANSFORMATION]
 for [HUGE AUDIENCE] by [SPECIFIC YEAR],
 creating a world where [BEAUTIFUL OUTCOME]."

════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
THE MISSION BUILDING FRAMEWORK
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

MISSION FORMULA (Choose your structure):

FORMULA A (Action + Who + Outcome):
"[VERB] [TARGET AUDIENCE] to [ACHIEVE OUTCOME]"
Example: "Empower entrepreneurs to build businesses they love"

FORMULA B (What + How + Why):
"[WHAT WE DO] through [HOW WE DO IT] so that [WHY IT MATTERS]"
Example: "We design financial tools through radical simplicity 
          so that money never stands between ideas and reality"

FORMULA C (Simple Power Statement):
"[BOLD CLAIM] for [AUDIENCE]"
Example: "World-class education for everyone, everywhere"

MISSION QUALITY CHECKLIST:
□ Can every employee recite it from memory?
□ Does it guide daily decisions?
□ Is it specific to YOUR company (not generic)?
□ Does it inspire AND direct at the same time?
□ Would it make sense in 5 years?

════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
THE VALUES ARCHITECTURE SYSTEM
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

VALUES = The rules of HOW you operate — non-negotiable

RULES FOR GREAT VALUES:
□ 3-7 values maximum (more = forgettable)
□ Each value must be behavioral (can be observed)
□ Each must be specific (not generic like "integrity")
□ Each should have a "we do X, not Y" anti-example
□ They must guide hiring, firing, and decision making

VALUE ARCHITECTURE FORMAT:
NAME: [1-3 word value name]
MEANING: [1 sentence — what this really means here]
IN ACTION: [Specific behavior this looks like]
NOT THIS: [Specific behavior this NEVER looks like]

EXAMPLE VALUE SET (Tech Startup):
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
VALUE 1: SHIP FAST, LEARN FASTER
Meaning: Speed of learning beats perfection every time
In Action: Release by Thursday. Gather feedback by Friday.
Not This: Waiting for perfect before sharing with users

VALUE 2: CUSTOMER TRUTH
Meaning: What customers do > what they say > what we think
In Action: Talk to 3 customers every week, minimum
Not This: Making product decisions from gut or meetings

VALUE 3: RADICAL TRANSPARENCY
Meaning: Share everything — numbers, failures, decisions
In Action: Monthly company-wide metrics review, no filters
Not This: Leaders knowing things the team doesn't

VALUE 4: EARN YOUR ROLE DAILY
Meaning: Your title is what you were. Your work is who you are.
In Action: Ownership mindset. No task is beneath you.
Not This: "That's not my job"

VALUE 5: BUILD FOR LEGACY
Meaning: Every decision: "Will this matter in 10 years?"
In Action: Long-term thinking in short-term execution
Not This: Sacrificing quality for quick quarterly numbers
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

PROMPT TO GENERATE YOUR VALUES:
"Act as a company culture architect. Based on this company 
[DESCRIPTION], target audience [ICP], and brand personality 
[ADJECTIVES], create 5 core company values using the format:
Name + Meaning + In Action + Not This. 
Values must be specific, behavioral, memorable, and authentic.
They must guide hiring, product, and operational decisions."
```

# 🎨 PART 2: LOGO CREATION SYSTEM

---

## 🔷 THE COMPLETE LOGO STRATEGY FRAMEWORK

```
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║                  UNIVERSAL LOGO CREATION SYSTEM                 ║
║       Works for every industry, budget, and brand style         ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝

════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
STEP 1: LOGO STRATEGY BEFORE DESIGN
(90% of bad logos fail here — strategy before aesthetics)
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

LOGO STRATEGY BRIEF (fill this before touching any design tool):

COMPANY:          ________________________________
INDUSTRY:         ________________________________
AUDIENCE:         ________________________________
PERSONALITY:      [Choose 3-5]
  □ Bold          □ Minimal      □ Playful      □ Serious
  □ Luxury        □ Friendly     □ Technical    □ Organic
  □ Elegant       □ Energetic    □ Trustworthy  □ Creative
  □ Professional  □ Disruptive   □ Warm         □ Cold
  
LOGO TYPE:        [Choose 1]
  □ Wordmark      → Name as designed text (Google, FedEx, Sony)
  □ Lettermark    → Initials only (IBM, HBO, NASA, LV)
  □ Brandmark     → Symbol only (Apple, Twitter, Nike tick)
  □ Combination   → Symbol + text (Amazon, Slack, Airbnb)
  □ Emblem        → Contained badge (Starbucks, NFL, Harvard)
  □ Mascot        → Character (Mailchimp, Reddit, KFC Colonel)
  □ Abstract Mark → Non-literal symbol (Pepsi, Adidas, Chase)

INDUSTRY COLOR PSYCHOLOGY:
  Tech/SaaS:      Blue, Indigo, Purple, Black, White
  Health:         Green, Teal, Blue, White
  Finance:        Navy, Dark Blue, Green, Black, Gold
  Food/Consumer:  Red, Orange, Yellow, Green
  Luxury:         Black, Gold, White, Deep Purple
  Education:      Blue, Green, Red, Yellow (classic)
  Creative/Media: Any — must be distinctive
  Environment:    Green, Earth tones, Blue
  Fashion:        Black, White, Minimal (usually)
  Sports:         Red, Black, Bold colors, High contrast

SHAPE PSYCHOLOGY:
  Circles/Curves  → Community, unity, wholeness, friendly
  Squares/Rect    → Stability, trust, order, professionalism
  Triangles       → Power, direction, movement, ambition
  Diagonals       → Dynamic, speed, innovation, progress
  Organic shapes  → Natural, human, authentic, approachable
  Geometric prec  → Technical, precise, modern, intelligent

════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
STEP 2: THE 5 LAWS OF TIMELESS LOGO DESIGN
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

LAW 1 → SIMPLICITY
  Rule: A 5-year-old should be able to draw it from memory
  Test: Sketch your logo concept with a pen in 30 seconds
  Why: Simple = Memorable = Recognizable = Brand Equity
  Great: Nike Swoosh, Apple, McDonald's M, Mercedes star
  Bad: Overdetailed, gradients, too many elements

LAW 2 → SCALABILITY  
  Rule: Must work at 16px (favicon) AND 16 feet (billboard)
  Test: Resize to 32x32px — still readable? Still recognizable?
  Why: Logo appears everywhere — app icon to truck wrap
  Fix: Create simplified icon version for small sizes

LAW 3 → VERSATILITY
  Rule: Must work in EVERY context and color situation
  Test: Does it work in: Full color / White / Black / 1 color?
  Why: Embroidery, stamps, legal docs, dark backgrounds
  Deliverables: Color / Reversed / White / Black / Grayscale

LAW 4 → DISTINCTIVENESS
  Rule: Must be immediately different from ALL competitors
  Test: The Police Lineup — 6 logos side by side, does yours stand out?
  Why: Recognition in a crowded market = business survival
  Research: Google "[industry] logos" — make yours different

LAW 5 → TIMELESSNESS
  Rule: Will it look great in 10-20 years?
  Test: Would this logo have looked good 10 years ago? 10 years ahead?
  Why: Redesigns cost recognition, trust, and millions
  Avoid: Trendy gradients, lens flares, drop shadows, web 2.0 gloss
  Timeless: Flat, geometric, classic, simple shapes

════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
STEP 3: AI LOGO CREATION PROMPTS
(Use with: Midjourney, Adobe Firefly, Ideogram, Recraft)
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

── MASTER LOGO PROMPT TEMPLATE ─────────────────────────────────

"Create a professional, timeless [LOGO TYPE] logo for a 
company called '[NAME]' in the [INDUSTRY] industry.

Brand personality: [ADJECTIVE 1], [ADJECTIVE 2], [ADJECTIVE 3]
Target audience: [WHO]
Color scheme: [PRIMARY COLOR] and [SECONDARY COLOR]
Style: Minimal, clean, vector-ready, flat design
Shape language: [Geometric/Organic/Abstract]

The logo must:
- Work on white AND dark backgrounds
- Be readable at small sizes
- Convey [CORE BRAND FEELING]
- Avoid gradients and drop shadows
- Feel [MODERN/CLASSIC/BOLD/MINIMAL]

Do NOT look like: [COMPETITOR 1], [COMPETITOR 2]
Should feel like (different industry, same energy): [INSPIRATION]

Format: White background, multiple variations,
        show primary + monochrome versions"

── MIDJOURNEY SPECIFIC PROMPT ───────────────────────────────────

/imagine minimal vector logo design for '[COMPANY NAME]', 
[INDUSTRY], [LOGO TYPE], [COLORS] color palette, [STYLE] style,
flat design, no gradients, scalable, professional, white background,
SVG quality, brand identity, geometric, timeless,
--style raw --ar 3:1 --v 6.1 --no shadows gradients

── IDEOGRAM PROMPT (best for text logos) ────────────────────────

A clean minimal wordmark logo for the company '[NAME]'.
Modern geometric sans-serif lettering, [COLOR] on white background.
Professional, scalable, flat vector style. No icons, just text.
The letterforms should feel [PERSONALITY ADJECTIVE].
Corporate identity design, high quality.

── ADOBE FIREFLY PROMPT ─────────────────────────────────────────

Professional brand logo, [LOGO TYPE] style, company name '[NAME]',
[INDUSTRY] sector, [STYLE DESCRIPTION], [COLOR] brand colors,
flat vector illustration, white background, commercial use ready,
clean modern design, scalable to any size

── RECRAFT PROMPT (vector-first AI) ─────────────────────────────

Vector logo for '[COMPANY NAME]', [INDUSTRY].
Style: [Minimalist/Geometric/Abstract/Illustrative]
Colors: [PRIMARY], [SECONDARY]
Mood: [BRAND ADJECTIVES]
Output: SVG-compatible, flat, no effects

════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
STEP 4: LOGO FILE DELIVERY SYSTEM
(What you need — no exceptions)
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

PRIMARY FILES (Master files — never lose these):
□ .SVG  → Web, scaling, editing (vector master)
□ .AI   → Adobe Illustrator source file
□ .EPS  → Universal print format
□ .PDF  → High-resolution print

RASTER EXPORTS (For specific use cases):
□ .PNG (transparent) → Web, presentations, social
□ .PNG (white bg)    → Documents, emails
□ .JPG               → Photos, backgrounds
□ .WEBP              → Modern web optimization

SIZE VARIATIONS:
□ Favicon: 16x16, 32x32, 180x180 (Apple touch icon)
□ Social: 400x400 (profile), 1200x630 (cover)
□ Print: 300 DPI minimum for all sizes
□ Email: 200-300px wide for email headers

LOGO VARIATIONS (every brand needs all of these):
□ Primary logo (horizontal — most used)
□ Stacked logo (vertical — square spaces)
□ Icon only (small spaces, app icons)
□ Wordmark only (text-heavy contexts)
□ Dark mode version (white/light on dark)
□ Single color (black, for legal/stamps)
□ Reversed (white, for dark backgrounds)
□ Grayscale (for grayscale printing)

LOGO USAGE RULES (document in brand guidelines):
□ Minimum size: Never smaller than [Xpx / Xmm]
□ Clear space: Always X units of space around logo
□ Forbidden: Never stretch, rotate, recolor, add effects
□ Background rules: Approved bg colors only
□ Proportion: Always scale proportionally (lock ratio)
```
